real Core RuntimeOwnerRoots controlBytes4096 reservation covers the fixed packed cursor shell; the nested ReadImage cursor has its own separate charged control. No payload bytes are retained in either shell. One Session moves the actual Arena and Usage; cursor, ReadImage cursor, native receipts and consumer tickets contain no Arena or retained Bytes. The source and arenaBinding are borrowed enclosing registrations; the native owner-cell association must name this actual resident moving Session, never a caller snapshot. Token equality alone is not same-Arena proof. Attachment registers a native lease naming the one already-owned image ticket without Heap.retain. Reader.held and ConsumerTicket are aliases in one aggregate. Raw payload lease and native image mapping are distinct. Only the matching actual attachment receipt creates a ResourceIO.ReadReply for nonempty data; empty EOF has no attachment or image. ResourceIO.consumeRead.release is retained as the same imageLease custody and delivered as Command.unregisterImage, never discarded as a new ticket. Full source consumer acknowledgment or actual consumer stop precedes native unregistration; only matching unregistered receipt permits source Heap.release/releaseStep. Native releaseImageLease only unregisters a borrowed mapping and never releases Heap roots. stopConsumer is an internal SOURCE-owner transfer, not host Wire implementation. AttachmentResult.stopped true means the pending registration is quiescent and created no mapping; a produced mapping must arrive as attached and be unregistered by receipt. Logical cancelled Reader settlement requires actual read quiescence and no pending ReadImage lease/payload, attachment or consumer, never merely cancellation intent. Every mutation delta is retained to acknowledgement. One stale incoming slot supplies callback backpressure; a further incoming outcome carries explicit custody and the enclosing dispatcher must retain it until requeued. No total-input cap or whole-host/native qualification
